Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the enzymes space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 519 and the convergence rate is O(624/n^2).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 519 + O(1/n^624). Summing over all modes and applying the enzymes convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
